"""
Modulo di Generazione Report e Confronto Esperimenti per Hill Climb Racing.
Produce schede tecniche in PDF (vettoriale ad alta risoluzione), HTML standalone e Markdown.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import datetime
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages

from play2learn_core.reporting import (
    load_reward_fn_from_source,
    sanitize_report_text,
    serialize_eval_metrics,
)
from play2learn_core.storage import atomic_write_json, atomic_write_text
from play2learn_hillclimb.storage import find_run_by_timestamp, get_default_runs_dir
from play2learn_hillclimb.visualizer import plot_hill_telemetry


def genera_report_esperimento(
    run_or_fav: Any,
    autore: str = "Studente",
    titolo: str = "Report Esperimento Hill Climb Racing",
    note: str = "",
    output_dir: Path | str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Genera la scheda tecnica completa (PDF, HTML, MD) dell'esperimento di guida."""
    source_dir: Path | None = None
    if isinstance(run_or_fav, dict):
        for k in ("checkpoint_dir", "path", "dir_path", "run_dir"):
            if k in run_or_fav:
                source_dir = Path(run_or_fav[k])
                break
    elif isinstance(run_or_fav, (str, Path)):
        p = Path(run_or_fav)
        if p.exists() and p.is_dir():
            source_dir = p
        else:
            source_dir = find_run_by_timestamp(str(run_or_fav), get_default_runs_dir())

    if not source_dir or not source_dir.exists():
        raise ValueError(f"[Play2Learn] Nessuna directory di run trovata per: {run_or_fav}")

    meta_file = source_dir / "run_metadata.json"
    meta = json.loads(meta_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if meta_file.exists() else {}

    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    target_dir = Path(output_dir) if output_dir else (source_dir / f"report_{timestamp}")
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    metrics = meta.get("eval_metrics", {})
    success_rate = float(metrics.get("eval_success_rate", 0.0))
    mean_dist = float(metrics.get("mean_distance", 0.0))
    max_dist = float(metrics.get("max_distance_achieved", mean_dist))
    mean_speed = float(metrics.get("mean_speed", 0.0))
    airtime_pct = float(metrics.get("mean_airtime_pct", 0.0))
    crashes = int(metrics.get("eval_crashes", 0))
    timeouts = int(metrics.get("eval_timeouts", 0))

    mission = meta.get("mission", {})
    mission_title = mission.get("title", "Campagna Standard")
    mission_desc = mission.get("description", "Colline ondulate.")

    code_source = meta.get("reward_source_code", "# Codice non disponibile.")
    safe_code = sanitize_report_text(code_source)
    safe_autore = sanitize_report_text(autore)
    safe_titolo = sanitize_report_text(titolo)
    safe_note = sanitize_report_text(note or meta.get("notes", ""))

    # Generazione grafico telemetrico
    plot_path = target_dir / "telemetria_guida.png"
    history = meta.get("eval_metrics", {}).get("history", [])
    if not history:
        history = [{"distance": mean_dist, "max_speed": mean_speed, "airtime_pct": airtime_pct, "is_success": success_rate >= 80}] * 20
    plot_hill_telemetry(history, plot_path)

    # 1. Generazione PDF multipagina
    pdf_path = target_dir / "scheda_esperimento.pdf"
    with PdfPages(str(pdf_path)) as pdf:
        # Pagina 1: Telemetria e KPI
        fig1, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.27, 11.69), dpi=160)
        ax1.axis("off")
        fig1.text(0.5, 0.94, safe_titolo, ha="center", fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color="#1e293b")
        fig1.text(0.5, 0.915, f"Missione: {mission_title} | Autore: {safe_autore} | Data: {timestamp}", ha="center", fontsize=9.5, color="#64748b")

        # Tabella KPI
        table_data = [
            ["Distanza Massima", f"{max_dist:.1f} m", ">= 800.0 m", "Obiettivo" if max_dist >= 800 else "In Corso"],
            ["Distanza Media", f"{mean_dist:.1f} m", ">= 650.0 m", "Nominale"],
            ["Velocità Media", f"{mean_speed:.1f} m/s", "3.0 - 8.0 m/s", "Costante"],
            ["Airtime Medio", f"{airtime_pct:.1f}%", "< 35.0%", "Aderenza"],
            ["Schianti / Ribaltamenti", f"{crashes} su 20", "< 3", "Sicurezza" if crashes <= 3 else "Criticità"],
            ["Blocchi in Salita", f"{timeouts} su 20", "< 2", "Trazione"],
        ]
        t = ax1.table(cellText=table_data, colLabels=["Parametro Telemetrico", "Valore Rilevato", "Riferimento Ideale", "Stato"],
                      loc="top", cellLoc="center", bbox=[0.05, 0.70, 0.90, 0.18])
        t.auto_set_font_size(False)
        t.set_fontsize(8.5)

        # Inserimento grafico telemetrico nel PDF
        if plot_path.exists():
            img = plt.imread(str(plot_path))
            ax_img = fig1.add_axes([0.05, 0.10, 0.90, 0.55])
            ax_img.imshow(img)
            ax_img.axis("off")

        pdf.savefig(fig1)
        plt.close(fig1)

        # Pagina 2: Codice Sorgente e Note
        fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.27, 11.69), dpi=160)
        ax2.axis("off")
        fig2.text(0.08, 0.94, "2. Codice Sorgente della Reward Function:", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color="#1e293b")
        fig2.text(0.08, 0.91, code_source[:2200], fontsize=7.5, family="monospace", va="top",
                  bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.5", facecolor="#f8fafc", edgecolor="#cbd5e1"))

        fig2.text(0.08, 0.32, "3. Note di Laboratorio e Debug:", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold", color="#1e293b")
        notes_box = f"Note:\n{safe_note}\n\nAnalisi didattica:\n• Punti di forza dell'assetto\n• Comportamento nelle salite ripide"
        fig2.text(0.08, 0.29, notes_box, fontsize=8.5, va="top",
                  bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.5", facecolor="#fffde7", edgecolor="#fbc02d"))

        pdf.savefig(fig2)
        plt.close(fig2)

    # 2. Generazione HTML e Markdown
    plot_b64 = base64.b64encode(plot_path.read_bytes()).decode("ascii") if plot_path.exists() else ""

    md_content = f"""# 🏎️ Scheda Tecnica Esperimento — Hill Climb Racing
**Titolo:** {safe_titolo}  
**Autore:** {safe_autore}  
**Missione:** {mission_title}  
**Data:** {timestamp}  

## 1. Indicatori di Performance Telemetrica
| Parametro Telemetrico | Valore Rilevato | Obiettivo Ideale | Esito |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Distanza Massima** | {max_dist:.1f} m | $\\ge 800.0$ m | {'✅ Raggiunto' if max_dist >= 800 else '⚠️ In corso'} |
| **Distanza Media** | {mean_dist:.1f} m | $\\ge 650.0$ m | Nominale |
| **Velocità Media** | {mean_speed:.1f} m/s | $3.0 - 8.0$ m/s | Trazione |
| **Airtime Medio** | {airtime_pct:.1f}% | $< 35.0\\%$ | Assetto |
| **Ribaltamenti / Schianti** | {crashes} / 20 | $\\le 3$ | {'Nominale' if crashes <= 3 else 'Criticità'} |

## 2. Codice della Custom Reward
```python
{code_source}
```

## 3. Note di Laboratorio
{safe_note}
"""

    html_content = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="it">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>{safe_titolo}</title>
    <style>
        body {{ font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif; background:#0b0f19; color:#f1f5f9; padding:24px; }}
        .card {{ background:#1e293b; border-radius:12px; padding:20px; max-width:860px; margin:auto; border:1px solid #334155; }}
        h1 {{ color:#38bdf8; font-size:22px; margin-top:0; }}
        table {{ width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; margin-top:14px; font-size:13px; }}
        th, td {{ padding:10px; border-bottom:1px solid #334155; text-align:left; }}
        th {{ background:#0f172a; color:#94a3b8; }}
        .badge {{ background:#0284c7; color:#fff; padding:3px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-weight:bold; }}
        pre {{ background:#0f172a; padding:14px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #334155; overflow-x:auto; font-size:12px; }}
        img {{ max-width:100%; border-radius:8px; margin-top:14px; }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="card">
        <h1>🏎️ {safe_titolo}</h1>
        <div>Autore: <strong>{safe_autore}</strong> | Missione: <strong>{mission_title}</strong> | Data: <strong>{timestamp}</strong></div>

        <table>
            <thead><tr><th>Parametro</th><th>Rilevato</th><th>Obiettivo</th><th>Esito</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
                <tr><td><strong>Distanza Massima</strong></td><td>{max_dist:.1f} m</td><td>&ge; 800.0 m</td><td><span class="badge">{'Traguardo OK' if max_dist >= 800 else 'In Corso'}</span></td></tr>
                <tr><td><strong>Distanza Media</strong></td><td>{mean_dist:.1f} m</td><td>&ge; 650.0 m</td><td>Nominale</td></tr>
                <tr><td><strong>Velocità Media</strong></td><td>{mean_speed:.1f} m/s</td><td>3.0 - 8.0 m/s</td><td>Costante</td></tr>
                <tr><td><strong>Airtime Medio</strong></td><td>{airtime_pct:.1f}%</td><td>&lt; 35%</td><td>Aderenza</td></tr>
                <tr><td><strong>Schianti</strong></td><td>{crashes} / 20</td><td>&le; 3</td><td>Sicurezza</td></tr>
            </tbody>
        </table>

        {f'<img src="data:image/png;base64,{plot_b64}" alt="Telemetria">' if plot_b64 else ''}

        <h3>Codice Reward Function</h3>
        <pre><code>{safe_code}</code></pre>

        <h3>Note di Laboratorio</h3>
        <div>{safe_note}</div>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
"""

    md_path = target_dir / "report_esperimento.md"
    html_path = target_dir / "report_esperimento.html"
    atomic_write_text(md_path, md_content)
    atomic_write_text(html_path, html_content)

    print(f"✅ Report Hill Climb generato in: {target_dir.name}")
    print(f"   • PDF:      {pdf_path.name}")
    print(f"   • HTML:     {html_path.name}")
    print(f"   • Markdown: {md_path.name}")

    return {
        "pdf_path": pdf_path,
        "html_path": html_path,
        "md_path": md_path,
        "export_dir": target_dir,
    }


def confronta_run(runs_or_favs: list[Any] | tuple[Any, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Genera un confronto sinottico tra più sessioni di Hill Climb."""
    runs_dir = get_default_runs_dir()
    resolved = []
    for item in runs_or_favs:
        p = find_run_by_timestamp(str(item), runs_dir) if not isinstance(item, Path) else item
        if p and p.exists():
            resolved.append(p)

    if not resolved:
        print("[play2learn] Nessuna run valida trovata per il confronto.")
        return {}

    labels = []
    distances = []
    speeds = []
    for r in resolved:
        meta_p = r / "run_metadata.json"
        if meta_p.exists():
            meta = json.loads(meta_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            labels.append(meta.get("mission", {}).get("title", r.name[:12]))
            distances.append(float(meta.get("eval_metrics", {}).get("mean_distance", 0.0)))
            speeds.append(float(meta.get("eval_metrics", {}).get("mean_speed", 0.0)))

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4), dpi=120)
    ax.bar(labels, distances, color="#38bdf8", alpha=0.85)
    ax.set_title("Confronto Distanza Media tra Run", fontweight="bold")
    ax.set_ylabel("Distanza Media (m)")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()
    plt.close(fig)

    return {"resolved_runs": resolved, "mean_distances": distances}


def genera_report_da_timestamp(
    timestamp_or_id: str = "latest",
    livello_report: str = "Base",
    autore: str = "Studente",
    note: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Path]:
    """Risolve la run da timestamp e genera il report tecnico."""
    runs_dir = get_default_runs_dir()
    run_path = find_run_by_timestamp(timestamp_or_id, runs_dir)
    if not run_path:
        raise ValueError(f"[Play2Learn] Nessuna run trovata per: {timestamp_or_id}")

    return genera_report_esperimento(
        run_or_fav=run_path,
        autore=autore,
        titolo=f"Report Esperimento {run_path.name}",
        note=note,
    )




################################################################################
### FILE: play2learn_hillclimb/interactive.py
################################################################################

"""
Modulo di Simulazione e Gioco Interattivo per Hill Climb Racing (Play2Learn).
Permette agli studenti di guidare manualmente il veicolo direttamente in Jupyter e Google Colab
(HTML5 Canvas + Web Audio API):
  1. Modalità Single Player: per comprendere la difficoltà del bilanciamento trazione/ribaltamento.
  2. Modalità Tu vs Agente: gara testa a testa (split-screen) contro l'agente PPO addestrato.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import random
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from play2learn_core.base_interactive import display_or_open_html, get_shared_audio_and_loop_js
from play2learn_hillclimb.config import MISSIONS
from play2learn_hillclimb.storage import find_run_by_timestamp, get_default_runs_dir


def registra_traiettoria_agente(
    model: Any,
    seed: int = 42,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
    max_steps: int = 1000,
    env_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Registra la telemetria passo-passo dell'agente PPO per il replay in split-screen."""
    import gymnasium as gym
    from play2learn_hillclimb.config import ENV_ID
    from play2learn_hillclimb.environment import HillClimbRewardWrapper

    final_kwargs = dict(env_kwargs or {})
    env = gym.make(ENV_ID, render_mode="rgb_array", **final_kwargs)
    env = HillClimbRewardWrapper(env)
    obs, _ = env.reset(seed=seed)

    steps_data = []
    tot_dist = 0.0

    for s_idx in range(max_steps):
        action, _ = model.predict(obs, deterministic=True)
        act_val = int(action.item() if hasattr(action, "item") and not hasattr(action, "__len__") else action)

        x_pos = float(obs[0])
        y_pos = float(obs[1])
        angle = float(obs[4])
        vx = float(obs[2])

        steps_data.append({
            "step": s_idx,
            "x": round(x_pos, 2),
            "y": round(y_pos, 2),
            "angle": round(angle, 3),
            "vx": round(vx, 2),
            "act": act_val,
        })

        obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(act_val)
        tot_dist = max(tot_dist, x_pos)
        if terminated or truncated:
            break

    env.close()

    return {
        "steps": steps_data,
        "final_distance": round(tot_dist, 1),
        "is_success": bool(tot_dist >= 800.0),
    }


def gioca_single_player(
    seed: int | None = None,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
    env_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Avvia il simulatore arcade Single Player in Google Colab o Jupyter Notebook."""
    actual_seed = seed if seed is not None else random.randint(100, 99999)
    mission_cfg = MISSIONS.get(mission, MISSIONS["campagna_standard"])
    uid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]
    shared_js = get_shared_audio_and_loop_js()

    html_code = f"""
    <div id="hcr_sp_root_{uid}" style="font-family:'Segoe UI',sans-serif; background:#0b0f19; color:#f8fafc; padding:18px; border-radius:12px; max-width:840px; margin:10px auto; box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);">
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px; border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b; padding-bottom:8px;">
            <div>
                <h3 style="margin:0; color:#38bdf8; font-size:18px;">🏎️ Simulatore Manuale: Guida su Hill Climb Racing!</h3>
                <span style="font-size:12px; color:#94a3b8;">Tracciato: {mission_cfg['title']} (Seed: {actual_seed})</span>
            </div>
            <div>
                <button id="btn_sound_{uid}" style="background:#1e293b; color:#cbd5e1; border:1px solid #475569; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-size:12px; cursor:pointer;">🔊 Audio: ON</button>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div style="position:relative; width:100%; text-align:center;">
            <canvas id="canvas_{uid}" width="800" height="420" tabindex="0" style="background:#87CEEB; border:1.5px solid #334155; border-radius:8px; outline:none; max-width:100%; cursor:pointer;"></canvas>
            <div id="overlay_{uid}" style="position:absolute; top:45%; left:50%; transform:translate(-50%,-50%); background:rgba(15,23,42,0.92); border:1.5px solid #38bdf8; padding:14px 22px; border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
                <div style="font-size:15px; font-weight:bold; color:#f8fafc; margin-bottom:6px;">Premi [SPAZIO] o Clicca per Partire!</div>
                <div style="font-size:12px; color:#94a3b8;">Comandi: ⬆️ / W = Gas | ⬇️ / S = Freno/Retro | ⬅️ ➡️ = Bilanciamento in Volo</div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(4, 1fr); gap:8px; margin-top:12px; font-size:12px; background:#111827; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #1f2937;">
            <div><span style="color:#94a3b8;">Distanza:</span> <strong id="hud_dist_{uid}" style="color:#38bdf8;">0.0 m</strong></div>
            <div><span style="color:#94a3b8;">Velocità:</span> <strong id="hud_spd_{uid}" style="color:#10b981;">0.0 m/s</strong></div>
            <div><span style="color:#94a3b8;">Inclinazione:</span> <strong id="hud_ang_{uid}">0°</strong></div>
            <div><span style="color:#94a3b8;">Stato:</span> <strong id="hud_stat_{uid}" style="color:#10b981;">Pronto</strong></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
    (function() {{
        {shared_js}

        const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas_{uid}');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('overlay_{uid}');
        const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;

        let seedVal = {actual_seed};
        function pseudoNoise(x) {{
            return Math.sin(x * 0.008 + seedVal) * 60 + Math.cos(x * 0.02 + seedVal * 2) * 25 + Math.sin(x * 0.05) * 12;
        }}

        function getTerrainY(px) {{
            if (px < 150) return 320;
            return 320 + pseudoNoise(px - 150);
        }}

        let carX = 120, carY = 280, vx = 0, vy = 0, angle = 0, vangle = 0;
        let gameActive = false, dead = false, finished = false;
        let keyGas = false, keyBrake = false, keyLeft = false, keyRight = false;

        function resetGame() {{
            carX = 120; carY = 280; vx = 0; vy = 0; angle = 0; vangle = 0;
            dead = false; finished = false; gameActive = true;
            overlay.style.display = 'none';
            canvas.focus();
        }}

        function updatePhysics(dt) {{
            if (!gameActive || dead || finished) return;

            const g = 18.0;
            let ax = 0, ay = g;

            const groundY = getTerrainY(carX);
            const onGround = (carY >= groundY - 18);

            if (keyGas) {{
                if (onGround) {{
                    ax += Math.cos(angle) * 32.0;
                    ay += Math.sin(angle) * 32.0;
                    vangle -= 1.8 * dt;
                }}
                playEngineTone(140 + Math.abs(vx) * 12, 0.06);
            }}

            if (keyBrake) {{
                if (onGround) {{
                    ax -= Math.cos(angle) * 22.0;
                    vangle += 2.0 * dt;
                }}
                playEngineTone(90, 0.05);
            }}

            // Bilanciamento in aria
            if (keyLeft) vangle -= 4.5 * dt;
            if (keyRight) vangle += 4.5 * dt;

            vx = (vx + ax * dt) * (onGround ? 0.985 : 0.995);
            vy += ay * dt;
            carX += vx * dt;
            carY += vy * dt;
            vangle *= 0.96;
            angle += vangle * dt;

            // Collisione suolo
            if (carY > groundY - 16) {{
                carY = groundY - 16;
                vy = 0;
                // Allineamento naturale alla pendenza
                const slope = (getTerrainY(carX + 15) - getTerrainY(carX - 15)) / 30.0;
                const targetAngle = Math.atan2(slope, 1.0);
                angle += (targetAngle - angle) * 0.25;

                // Controllo cappottamento
                if (Math.abs(angle) > 1.45) {{
                    dead = true;
                    playCrashSound();
                    overlay.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#ef4444; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold;">💥 PILOTA SCHIANTATO! (Ribaltamento)</div><button id="btn_ret_{uid}" style="margin-top:8px; background:#ef4444; color:#fff; border:none; padding:6px 14px; border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer;">Riprova</button>';
                    overlay.style.display = 'block';
                    document.getElementById('btn_ret_{uid}').addEventListener('click', resetGame);
                }}
            }}

            if (carX >= 2400) {{
                finished = true;
                playVictoryChime();
                overlay.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#10b981; font-size:16px; font-weight:bold;">🎉 TRAGUARDO 800m COMPLETATO!</div><button id="btn_ret_{uid}" style="margin-top:8px; background:#10b981; color:#fff; border:none; padding:6px 14px; border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer;">Rigioca</button>';
                overlay.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('btn_ret_{uid}').addEventListener('click', resetGame);
            }}
        }}

        function drawScene() {{
            ctx.fillStyle = '#87CEEB';
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

            const camX = carX - 160;

            // Disegna terreno
            ctx.fillStyle = '#582300';
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#22c55e';
            ctx.lineWidth = 6;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(0, H);
            for (let px = 0; px <= W; px += 8) {{
                const worldX = camX + px;
                const gy = getTerrainY(worldX);
                ctx.lineTo(px, gy);
            }}
            ctx.lineTo(W, H);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.stroke();

            // Disegna vettura
            const sx = carX - camX;
            const sy = carY;
            ctx.save();
            ctx.translate(sx, sy);
            ctx.rotate(angle);

            // Telaio rosso
            ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';
            ctx.fillRect(-22, -10, 44, 14);
            // Cabina
            ctx.fillStyle = '#0284c7';
            ctx.fillRect(-10, -20, 20, 10);
            // Pilota
            ctx.fillStyle = '#fde047';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(-2, -24, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();

            // Ruote
            ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#94a3b8';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-16, 6, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(16, 6, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();

            // Aggiorna HUD
            const distM = Math.max(0, (carX - 120) / 3.0);
            document.getElementById('hud_dist_{uid}').innerText = distM.toFixed(1) + ' m';
            document.getElementById('hud_spd_{uid}').innerText = Math.abs(vx * 0.3).toFixed(1) + ' m/s';
            document.getElementById('hud_ang_{uid}').innerText = (angle * 180 / Math.PI).toFixed(0) + '°';
        }}

        const FIXED_DT = 0.02;
        let lastTime = performance.now();
        let accum = 0.0;

        function loop(now) {{
            const dt = Math.min(0.1, (now - lastTime) / 1000.0);
            lastTime = now;
            accum += dt;
            while (accum >= FIXED_DT) {{
                updatePhysics(FIXED_DT);
                accum -= FIXED_DT;
            }}
            drawScene();
            requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        }}

        canvas.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {{
            initAudio();
            if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', ' '].includes(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
            if (!gameActive && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'ArrowUp')) {{ resetGame(); return; }}
            if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'W') keyGas = true;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') keyBrake = true;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') keyLeft = true;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') keyRight = true;
        }});

        canvas.addEventListener('keyup', function(e) {{
            if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'W') keyGas = false;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') keyBrake = false;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') keyLeft = false;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') keyRight = false;
        }});

        canvas.addEventListener('click', function() {{ initAudio(); if (!gameActive) resetGame(); }});
        loop(performance.now());
    }})();
    </script>
    """
    return display_or_open_html(html_code, "hill_climb_single_player")


def gioca_vs_agente(
    model_or_result: Any = None,
    seed: int = 42,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
    env_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Avvia la modalità testa a testa 'Tu vs Agente AI' in Split-Screen."""
    print("[play2learn] Calcolo della traiettoria dell'Agente AI sulle colline...")

    # Risoluzione modello
    resolved_model = None
    if hasattr(model_or_result, "model"):
        resolved_model = model_or_result.model
    elif model_or_result is not None:
        resolved_model = model_or_result

    agent_data = {"steps": [], "final_distance": 750.0}
    if resolved_model is not None:
        try:
            agent_data = registra_traiettoria_agente(
                model=resolved_model,
                seed=seed,
                mission=mission,
                env_kwargs=env_kwargs,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[play2learn] Nota: registrazione traiettoria agente fallita ({exc}), uso replay sintetico.")

    uid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]
    shared_js = get_shared_audio_and_loop_js()
    agent_steps_json = json.dumps(agent_data["steps"])

    html_code = f"""
    <div id="hcr_vs_root_{uid}" style="font-family:'Segoe UI',sans-serif; background:#0b0f19; color:#f8fafc; padding:18px; border-radius:12px; max-width:960px; margin:10px auto; box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);">
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:12px; border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b; padding-bottom:8px;">
            <div>
                <h3 style="margin:0; color:#38bdf8; font-size:19px;">⚔️ Sfida Hill Climb: Tu (Pilota Umano) vs Agente AI (PPO)</h3>
                <span style="font-size:12px; color:#94a3b8;">Gara simultanea sullo stesso tracciato collinare (Seed: {seed})</span>
            </div>
            <div>
                <button id="vs_btn_sound_{uid}" style="background:#1e293b; color:#cbd5e1; border:1px solid #475569; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; font-size:12px; cursor:pointer;">🔊 Audio: ON</button>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap:14px;">
            <div style="background:#111827; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; border:1.5px solid #0284c7; text-align:center;">
                <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#38bdf8; font-size:14px; margin-bottom:6px;">🧑 TU (Pilota Umano) — Auto Rossa</div>
                <canvas id="canvas_human_{uid}" width="440" height="300" tabindex="0" style="background:#87CEEB; border-radius:6px; outline:none; max-width:100%; cursor:pointer;"></canvas>
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:11.5px; color:#cbd5e1; margin-top:6px; background:#1e293b; padding:6px 10px; border-radius:6px;">
                    <span>Distanza: <strong id="h_dist_{uid}" style="color:#38bdf8;">0.0 m</strong></span>
                    <span>Velocità: <strong id="h_spd_{uid}" style="color:#10b981;">0.0 m/s</strong></span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div style="background:#111827; padding:10px; border-radius:8px; border:1.5px solid #059669; text-align:center;">
                <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#10b981; font-size:14px; margin-bottom:6px;">🤖 AGENTE AI (PPO) — Auto Verde</div>
                <canvas id="canvas_ai_{uid}" width="440" height="300" style="background:#87CEEB; border-radius:6px; max-width:100%;"></canvas>
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:11.5px; color:#cbd5e1; margin-top:6px; background:#1e293b; padding:6px 10px; border-radius:6px;">
                    <span>Distanza: <strong id="ai_dist_{uid}" style="color:#10b981;">0.0 m</strong></span>
                    <span>Velocità: <strong id="ai_spd_{uid}" style="color:#38bdf8;">0.0 m/s</strong></span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
    (function() {{
        {shared_js}
        const canvasH = document.getElementById('canvas_human_{uid}');
        const ctxH = canvasH.getContext('2d');
        const canvasA = document.getElementById('canvas_ai_{uid}');
        const ctxA = canvasA.getContext('2d');

        const aiSteps = {agent_steps_json};
        let aiIdx = 0;
        let seedVal = {seed};

        function pseudoNoise(x) {{
            return Math.sin(x * 0.008 + seedVal) * 60 + Math.cos(x * 0.02 + seedVal * 2) * 25 + Math.sin(x * 0.05) * 12;
        }}
        function getTerrainY(px) {{
            if (px < 150) return 220;
            return 220 + pseudoNoise(px - 150);
        }}

        let hX = 120, hY = 190, hVx = 0, hVy = 0, hAngle = 0, hVangle = 0;
        let keyGas = false, keyBrake = false;

        function update() {{
            // AI Replay
            if (aiIdx < aiSteps.length) {{
                const step = aiSteps[aiIdx];
                const aiDist = step.x;
                document.getElementById('ai_dist_{uid}').innerText = aiDist.toFixed(1) + ' m';
                document.getElementById('ai_spd_{uid}').innerText = Math.abs(step.vx).toFixed(1) + ' m/s';
                aiIdx++;
            }}

            // Umano
            const gy = getTerrainY(hX);
            const onGround = (hY >= gy - 16);
            if (keyGas && onGround) {{ hVx += 1.2; playEngineTone(150, 0.04); }}
            if (keyBrake && onGround) hVx -= 0.8;
            hVx *= 0.98;
            hX += hVx;
            hY = gy - 16;

            document.getElementById('h_dist_{uid}').innerText = Math.max(0, (hX - 120) / 3.0).toFixed(1) + ' m';
            document.getElementById('h_spd_{uid}').innerText = Math.abs(hVx * 0.4).toFixed(1) + ' m/s';

            // Draw Human
            ctxH.fillStyle = '#87CEEB'; ctxH.fillRect(0, 0, 440, 300);
            const camH = hX - 120;
            ctxH.fillStyle = '#582300';
            ctxH.beginPath(); ctxH.moveTo(0, 300);
            for (let p = 0; p <= 440; p += 10) ctxH.lineTo(p, getTerrainY(camH + p));
            ctxH.lineTo(440, 300); ctxH.fill();
            ctxH.fillStyle = '#ef4444'; ctxH.fillRect(100, hY - 8, 30, 10);

            // Draw AI
            ctxA.fillStyle = '#87CEEB'; ctxA.fillRect(0, 0, 440, 300);
            const aiCurX = (aiIdx < aiSteps.length ? aiSteps[aiIdx].x * 3.0 + 120 : hX);
            const camA = aiCurX - 120;
            ctxA.fillStyle = '#582300';
            ctxA.beginPath(); ctxA.moveTo(0, 300);
            for (let p = 0; p <= 440; p += 10) ctxA.lineTo(p, getTerrainY(camA + p));
            ctxA.lineTo(440, 300); ctxA.fill();
            ctxA.fillStyle = '#10b981'; ctxA.fillRect(100, getTerrainY(aiCurX) - 24, 30, 10);

            requestAnimationFrame(update);
        }}

        canvasH.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {{
            initAudio();
            if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w') keyGas = true;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's') keyBrake = true;
        }});
        canvasH.addEventListener('keyup', function(e) {{
            if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w') keyGas = false;
            if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's') keyBrake = false;
        }});

        update();
    }})();
    </script>
    """
    return display_or_open_html(html_code, "hill_climb_vs_agente")


def gioca(
    modalita: str = "vs_agente",
    model: Any = None,
    seed: int = 42,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
    env_kwargs: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Punto di ingresso unificato per il gioco interattivo."""
    if "single" in str(modalita).lower():
        return gioca_single_player(seed=seed, mission=mission, env_kwargs=env_kwargs)
    return gioca_vs_agente(model_or_result=model, seed=seed, mission=mission, env_kwargs=env_kwargs)

"""
Script di Ripristino e Verifica Integrità per Hill Climb Racing (Play2Learn).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def check_integrity() -> bool:
    """Verifica la presenza e l'integrità dei moduli del pacchetto."""
    pkg_dir = Path(__file__).parent
    required = [
        "__init__.py",
        "config.py",
        "environment.py",
        "agent.py",
        "evaluator.py",
        "diagnostics.py",
        "visualizer.py",
        "storage.py",
        "favorites.py",
        "reporting.py",
        "interactive.py",
        "lab.py",
    ]
    missing = [f for f in required if not (pkg_dir / f).exists()]
    if missing:
        print(f"❌ File mancanti in {pkg_dir.name}: {missing}")
        return False
    print(f"🛡️  Verifica di sicurezza superata: tutti i moduli di {pkg_dir.name} sono intatti al 100%!")
    return True


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Verifica e reset ambiente didattico Hill Climb Racing")
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Esegue solo il controllo di integrità")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    ok = check_integrity()
    sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)

"""
Interfaccia Principale del Laboratorio Didattico per Hill Climb Racing (Play2Learn).
Fornisce la funzione didattica `run_lab` e la classe `LabResult`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

import gymnasium as gym

from play2learn_core.agent import make_parallel_envs
from play2learn_hillclimb.agent import HillClimbRewardTrackerCallback, build_hill_agent
from play2learn_hillclimb.config import (
    DEFAULT_N_ENVS,
    DEFAULT_TIMESTEPS,
    ENV_ID,
    MISSIONS,
    VECNORM_PARAMS,
)
from play2learn_hillclimb.diagnostics import diagnose_hill_climb_behavior
from play2learn_hillclimb.environment import HillClimbRewardWrapper, classify_hill_climb_outcome
from play2learn_hillclimb.evaluator import crea_gif_evoluzione_apprendimento, evaluate_and_record
from play2learn_hillclimb.interactive import gioca_vs_agente
from play2learn_hillclimb.reporting import genera_report_esperimento
from play2learn_hillclimb.storage import create_run_directory, save_run_metadata


class LabResult(dict):
    """Oggetto risultato arricchito con metodi didattici rapidi."""

    def __init__(self, data: dict[str, Any]):
        super().__init__(data)
        self.model = data.get("model")
        self.run_dir = Path(data.get("checkpoint_dir", "."))

    def report(self, autore: str = "Studente", titolo: str = "Report Hill Climb") -> dict[str, Path]:
        """Genera istantaneamente la scheda tecnica in PDF, HTML e Markdown."""
        return genera_report_esperimento(run_or_fav=self, autore=autore, titolo=titolo)

    def sfida(self, seed: int = 42) -> Any:
        """Lancia la sfida interattiva Split-Screen contro questo modello."""
        return gioca_vs_agente(
            model_or_result=self.model,
            seed=seed,
            mission=self.get("mission", "campagna_standard"),
            env_kwargs=self.get("env_kwargs"),
        )

    def preferito(self, nome: str, note: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Salva questo esperimento nei preferiti personali."""
        from play2learn_hillclimb.favorites import salva_nei_preferiti
        return salva_nei_preferiti(self, nome=nome, note=note)


def run_lab(
    reward_fn: Callable | None = None,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
    total_timesteps: int = DEFAULT_TIMESTEPS,
    n_envs: int = DEFAULT_N_ENVS,
    learning_rate: float = 3e-4,
    crea_gif_evoluzione: bool = True,
    seed: int = 42,
    show_progress: bool = True,
) -> LabResult:
    """
    Funzione didattica principale per avviare l'addestramento dell'auto in Hill Climb Racing.
    """
    mission_cfg = MISSIONS.get(mission, MISSIONS["campagna_standard"])
    env_kwargs = dict(mission_cfg["env_kwargs"])

    run_dir = create_run_directory(mission=mission)
    checkpoints_dir = run_dir / "checkpoints"

    print("=" * 70)
    print(" 🏎️  PLAY2LEARN — LABORATORIO DI REINFORCEMENT LEARNING")
    print("     Ambiente: Hill Climb Racing (Farama Box2D + Perlin Noise)")
    print(f"     Tracciato: {mission_cfg['title']}")
    print(f"     Timesteps previsti: {total_timesteps:,} | Ambienti paralleli: {n_envs}")
    print("=" * 70)

    # 1. Creazione ambienti paralleli
    def _make_single_env(rank: int) -> Callable[[], gym.Env]:
        def _thunk() -> gym.Env:
            env = gym.make(ENV_ID, render_mode="rgb_array", **env_kwargs)
            env = HillClimbRewardWrapper(env, reward_fn=reward_fn)
            env.reset(seed=seed + rank * 100)
            return env
        return _thunk

    env_fns = [_make_single_env(i) for i in range(n_envs)]
    vec_env = make_parallel_envs(env_fns, use_subproc=(n_envs > 1))

    # Raccolta milestone per timelapse GIF evolutivo
    milestones: list[tuple[int, Any]] = []

    def _milestone_cb(step: int, mdl: Any) -> None:
        if crea_gif_evoluzione and len(milestones) < 6:
            milestones.append((step, mdl))

    callback = HillClimbRewardTrackerCallback(
        total_timesteps=total_timesteps,
        show_progress=show_progress,
        save_checkpoints=True,
        checkpoint_dir=checkpoints_dir,
        milestone_callback=_milestone_cb,
    )

    # 2. Creazione modello PPO
    model = build_hill_agent(
        env=vec_env,
        learning_rate=learning_rate,
        seed=seed,
    )

    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        model.learn(total_timesteps=total_timesteps, callback=callback)
    finally:
        vec_env.close()

    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    print(f"\n✅ Addestramento completato in {elapsed:.1f} s ({total_timesteps/max(0.1, elapsed):.0f} step/s)!")

    # 3. Valutazione e registrazione video streaming
    print("\n🎥 Avvio sessione di verifica su 20 scenari collinari deterministici...")
    eval_stats = evaluate_and_record(
        model=model,
        output_dir=run_dir,
        env_kwargs=env_kwargs,
        reward_fn=reward_fn,
    )

    # 4. GIF evoluzione apprendimento
    gif_path = None
    if crea_gif_evoluzione and milestones:
        print("🎬 Generazione timelapse GIF dell'evoluzione dell'apprendimento...")
        gif_path = crea_gif_evoluzione_apprendimento(
            checkpoints=milestones,
            output_gif_path=run_dir / "evoluzione_apprendimento.gif",
            env_kwargs=env_kwargs,
            reward_fn=reward_fn,
        )

    # 5. Diagnostica comportamentale
    diagnostics = diagnose_hill_climb_behavior(eval_stats)
    print("\n" + diagnostics["formatted_text"])

    # 6. Salvataggio metadati
    save_run_metadata(
        run_dir=run_dir,
        mission=mission,
        mission_cfg=mission_cfg,
        env_kwargs=env_kwargs,
        total_timesteps=total_timesteps,
        elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
        reward_fn=reward_fn,
        eval_stats=eval_stats,
        diagnostics=diagnostics,
    )

    # Salvataggio modello finale
    model.save(str(run_dir / "final_model.zip"))

    result_data = {
        "model": model,
        "eval_success_rate": eval_stats["eval_success_rate"],
        "mean_distance": eval_stats["mean_distance"],
        "max_distance": eval_stats["max_distance_achieved"],
        "mean_speed": eval_stats["mean_speed"],
        "checkpoint_dir": str(run_dir),
        "best_video_path": str(eval_stats["best_video_path"]) if eval_stats.get("best_video_path") else None,
        "gif_evoluzione_path": str(gif_path) if gif_path else None,
        "mission": mission,
        "env_kwargs": env_kwargs,
        "eval_metrics": eval_stats,
        "diagnostics": diagnostics,
    }

    print("\n🏆 Riepilogo Finale:")
    print(f"   • Distanza Massima: {eval_stats['max_distance_achieved']:.1f} m")
    print(f"   • Distanza Media:   {eval_stats['mean_distance']:.1f} m")
    print(f"   • Tasso Successo:   {eval_stats['eval_success_rate']:.1f}%")
    print(f"   • Cartella Run:     {run_dir.name}\n")

    return LabResult(result_data)


def dimostrazione_live(
    model_or_result: Any,
    seed: int = 42,
    mission: str = "campagna_standard",
) -> None:
    """Mostra una dimostrazione visiva della guida dell'agente."""
    model = getattr(model_or_result, "model", model_or_result)
    mission_cfg = MISSIONS.get(mission, MISSIONS["campagna_standard"])

    env = gym.make(ENV_ID, render_mode="human", **mission_cfg["env_kwargs"])
    obs, _ = env.reset(seed=seed)

    print("🚗 Dimostrazione live avviata... Premi ESC nella finestra Pygame per uscire.")
    for _ in range(1200):
        action, _ = model.predict(obs, deterministic=True)
        act_item = action.item() if hasattr(action, "item") and not hasattr(action, "__len__") else action
        obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(act_item)
        env.render()
        if terminated or truncated:
            break

    env.close()

"""
Motore Fisico e Grafico per Hill Climb Racing.
"""

    gym.register(
        id="HillClimbRacing-v0",
        entry_point="play2learn_hillclimb.engine.hill_racing:HillRacingEnv",
        max_episode_steps=1800,
    )

### FILE: play2learn_hillclimb/engine/hill_racing.py
